# Storyboard · TD and MIS (Postal Exams, POSB)

No character in this channel: no host rig, no person drawn. Every scene is an animated lecture slide on the cream canvas (#fbf7ee) under the app's fixed frame (grey section label top left, red series pill top right; never drawn by the scene, never animated). Content area: x 100–1820, y 150–1000 (the header owns y 0–130). Postal terms in English (Inter bold, 88–120 px) with the Hindi translation beneath (Noto Sans Devanagari, 48–60 px, warm grey or red); everything else Hindi only, never under 44 px. Max five items on screen; earlier points fade to #6f6a63 when a screen gets busy. A new picture or a new build about every 4.5 s; key facts hold ≥ 4 s; a rule card holds through its whole explanation with highlights moving through it. Moves used: draw, write, pop, fade, slide, type, count, grow, move, pulse, color, out, words, tracking, reveal, stamp, highlight, underline, strike, swap. Never: smear, ticker, scatter, spin, float, wiggle, shake, bounce, flip, letters.

## Shared

Series tag: POSB (every scene).

Section labels (English, by chapter):
- s001–s014: INTRO · TD AND MIS
- s015–s027: PART 1 · WHO CAN OPEN
- s028–s030: PART 1 · THE LAW

Shared pieces (each made once; scenes use them with one scale number):
- `td-passbook-white` (new): the TD passbook SB-5A, 240×320, white cover with 3 px ink border, thin #ead9a8 inner line, a blank cream label panel (x 62–202, y 70–154), lower right corner lifted showing cream pages; no lettering (scene writes "TD · SB-5A" on the label). Anchors base, top, grip. The recurring TD icon whenever the lecture switches to TD. Scenes: s001, s005, s007, s011, s017, s026, s036, s039, s040, s052–s055, s058–s061, s068, s071, s073, s077, s080, s081, s085, s087, s088, s091, s093, s096, s098, s108, s111, s177, s196, s205, s212, s213, s223, s236, s242, s246, s251.
- `passbook` (library, reuse): red closed passbook 240×320 with blank label; the MIS passbook SB-5 (scene writes "MIS · SB-5"). The recurring MIS icon. Scenes: s001, s005, s007, s008, s009, s011, s017, s021, s026, s109, s140, s142, s144, s146, s147, s150, s153, s158, s159, s169, s172, s173, s176, s177, s185, s186, s194–s196, s198, s199, s203, s205, s211, s225, s236, s242, s248, s251.
- `stamp-term-card` (new): blank postal-red #9e1b1b card 760×380 with a perforated stamp edge and a thin #b8403a inner line, a white inner panel optional (#window, 640×120 at the bottom); no lettering. The key-term card: English term in white, Hindi in pale yellow #fde9a6 beneath. Anchors base, top. Scenes: s003, s007, s017, s031, s037–s039, s061, s075, s096, s104, s111, s113, s128, s145, s159, s168, s171, s175, s191, s194, s200, s214, s221, s223, s245.
- `rupee-note-bundle` (new): a flat ink bundle of rupee notes 320×200 (sand notes, a red paper band round the middle with a small ₹ in a circle), 3 px ink; no amounts. Anchors base, top, grip. The lump sum / principal object. Scenes: s002, s003, s009, s010, s040, s060, s061, s104, s111, s162, s195, s199, s201, s202.
- `rupee-coin` (new): one flat gold-sand coin 120×120 with an ink ₹, 3 px ink; scenes repeat it for interest drops (monthly MIS interest, annual TD interest). Anchors base, top. Scenes: s003, s006, s009, s044, s046, s052, s054, s057, s058, s060, s061, s072, s105, s158, s159, s169, s176, s195, s239.
- `calendar-page` (library, reuse): tear-off page for a single date (maturity date, 10 November 2023, 20 October). Scenes: s004, s043, s046, s051, s060, s063, s068, s098, s101, s104, s166, s167, s224.
- `calendar-month-page` (library, reuse): a month grid for due dates and refund windows (31 Jan → last date of Feb; 10 Aug → 30 Sep; refund in October → interest to September). Scenes: s026, s155, s165, s166, s167, s202.
- `calendar-quarters` (library, reuse): the year in four quarter blocks for quarterly compounding. Scenes: s041, s044, s095.
- `person-icons` (library, reuse): adult / child / guardian / group figures for who can open and joint holders. Scenes: s015, s017–s019, s021, s031, s144, s146, s150, s152, s244.
- `complaint-form-sheet` (library, reuse) as the generic blank form: red header band for the form's name (Form-1, Form-2, SB-7A, SB-7B, GSPR Form-5, Form-11, Form-10, SB-83, ECS-1, Form 97, Form 121). Scenes: s055, s059, s064, s086, s087, s112, s124, s173, s174, s186, s195, s201, s205, s206, s210, s215, s220, s224, s229, s231, s242.
- `cheque-leaf` (library, reuse): cancelled cheque / crossed cheque. Scenes: s056, s057, s174, s175, s218.
- `phone-transfer` (library, reuse): the credit going to the linked account. Scenes: s054, s172.
- `browser-window` (library, reuse): Internet Banking MIS closure steps. Scenes: s225, s226, s228.
- `signature-slip` (library, reuse): signed receipt / signature check. Scenes: s122, s215, s217.
- `pda-passbook` (library, reuse): an open passbook ledger for endorsement and records. Scenes: s056, s117, s120, s174, s195.
- set `citizen-counter` (library, reuse): the post-office counter window, used empty (no person) for the closing line; built from the library piece `citizen-counter-window`. Spots window, sign, counter-left, counter-right, counter-centre; framings wide / medium / close. Scenes: s251.

Components used as starting points (moulded per scene): chapter-title (part openers), key-term, compare-cards, compare-table, data-chart (rates), formula-card (worked sums), timeline (dates), step-list (procedures, internet banking), exam-trap (trap lines), myth-fact (wrong options), quote-card / rule-card (document cards), mind-map (the 10-part map), recap-card (recaps).

Real photos (all optional; each scene works with its drawing if the photo isn't found): `post-office-savings-counter`, `india-post-savings-passbook`, `blank-account-opening-form` (see materials.json).

## Continuity

- No character anywhere. One world: the cream paper deck. The two recurring scheme icons keep their colours everywhere: TD = white passbook (td-passbook-white) on the LEFT, MIS = red passbook on the RIGHT whenever both appear (screen direction: TD left, MIS right, for every compare card, table column and recap row).
- Inside a chapter, scenes cut (`cut`); a scene that keeps the previous card on screen says `Continues: sNNN` and starts with that card exactly as it ended. A new chapter (section label changes) arrives with `wipe` from the right (0.6 s); recaps arrive with `fade` (0.5 s). The first scene (s001) is a `cut` with the hook already on screen.
- Colour meanings never change: red = cards and headings; yellow = highlight / what's added; green = allowed / correct / ticks; slate strike + cross = wrong option; grey = supporting.
- Rates always carry "1 Oct–31 Dec 2026 · Source: SB Order 12/2026" beneath; limits and fees carry their scheme or SB Order beneath.
- Shared state: none changes across scenes (state.json is {}).
